Gradient of LSE:
compute c as array length 2n-1. m = max(c). w = exp((c-m)/τ) (if τ>0). w_sum = sum(w). λ = w / w_sum (probability over coefficients, weights according to soft max). For τ small, concentrates on max. Gradient of L = λ dot c; ∂L/∂p_j = (convolve(λ, p)[j]) + λ_{2j} p_j (if 2j within range). This derivative is positive. We want minimize L subject sum p=1, p>0. Use projected gradient: p_new = p - η grad; normalize. But grad may push negative; use log parameterization: q = log p, p=softmax(q). Gradient in q? For simplex smooth, using p projection easier; but can hit zero. Use multiplicative update: p_new = p * exp(-η grad) / sum. This is mirror descent with KL, stays positive. Since objective not convex, but likely. Need choose η scale. p_i ~1/n. grad magnitude? For LSE of c (scale ~1/n), derivative ~ λ_k p_{...} sum over k ~ O(1/n). η maybe n (1000) to effect O(1). Or use gradient of score=2n L, scale independent; γ=η*2n maybe O(1). Let's derive typical p~1/n, c diff scale 1/n, grad ~1/n. To change p relative O(0.1), need η ~0.1n. Set step param s=0.1..1.0: p = p * exp(-s*grad*p? no). If grad ~1/n, exp(-s/n) negligible for s=1. Need grad scaled by n: use normalized gradient g = n*grad ~O(1). Then p*=exp(-s*g). Choose s around 0.05-0.5. Compute L with c scale? Could optimize objective directly: use w = softmax(c/τ). If τ chosen, w probability. grad ~1/n. Multiply by scale α = n? We can tune.

Alternatively use subgradient of max: active coefficients set, λ uniform over near max. Then gradient similar. We can compute active = c > max - tol, λ=1/count. Then p *= exp(-s*g). This is straightforward and less cost.

Detailed implementation plan:

Globals: no closures. Use time, np, scipy maybe not necessary.

Functions:
- next_fast_len(m): power of two >=m (np.fft not need but use 2**ceil).
- fftconvolve_self(p, n, L): compute c length 2n-1. Use np.fft.rfft? Simpler use fft complex to correlate? For self convolution, use F = np.fft.rfft(p, L); c = np.fft.irfft(F*F, L)[:2*n-1]. Because real input. For gradient h = ifft(Fλ*conj(Fp)), need full complex fft (not rfft) because conj(Fp). Could use full fft for all. L power of 2. Use np.fft.fft.

- eval_score(p): n=len(p); sump = np.sum(p); if sump<=0 return inf; c = conv_self; maxc = max(c.real); score = 2*n*maxc/(sump*sump). Since p nonnegative; due roundoff clip c negative? max might negative if all zero? Use max(0). Return float.

- objective_smooth(p, tau): maybe return LSE and weights.

- mul_update(p, weights, step): grad = gradient_from_weights(p, n, weights); g = grad * n (scale); p2 = p * np.exp(-step * g); normalize; return.

But gradient_from_weights uses FFT of weights length L. Weights λ over c length 2n-1 padded. Need set λ array length L. Fp = fft(p). Fλ=fft(λ). h=ifft(Fλ * conj(Fp)).real[:n] + p * λ[2*np.arange(n)]. Wait λ[2j] for j=0..n-1; 2j max 2n-2. Good.

But if using LSE weights, λ sums1. Need maybe subtract mean gradient? Multiplicative normalization handles sum, no need.

Step size: We can try adaptive. Let initial p normalized sum1. Compute c. Use τ maybe multiple: tau_rel = cmax * tau_factor. If cmax=0 (zero) inf. cmax ~score/(2n) ~ maybe 0.8/n. We can optimize both hard max (tau=0) and soft. Hard: active = c >= maxc - tol (tol maybe 1e-4*maxc or 1e-8). λ uniform active. Soft: LSE likely better.

Search strategy in time:
1. Generate a set of initial profiles for a few lengths. Evaluate local score. Keep global best.
2. For each profile, run a multiplicative gradient local optimizer with schedule of tau factors and step sizes. Use time slices.
3. Periodically do random restarts from current best with perturbations.
4. At end, maybe do coordinate fine-tuning on best using local exact? Return best.

Need implement no closures. All helper functions top-level.

Initial profiles:
- uniform: ones/n.
- ramp: x from0 to1 maybe normalized, ensure positive: a = 0.1 + x (or x+ε), normalize.
- reverse ramp.
- two-step: first half base 1, second half ratio r (or vice versa). Use r=2 or 0.5 normalized.
- three-step: weights maybe [1,1.5,2] or random.
- sine odd: 1 + A*sin(2πx) with A up to 1; normalize; ensure nonneg. Maybe A=0.5,0.7,0.9.
- cosine harmonic: 1 + A*sin(2π(2k+1)x) etc.
- step smoothed: 1/(1+exp(-k(x-0.5))) normalized with mean maybe not; ensure nonneg; subtract? Actually raw values 0.1 to1.9.
- Gaussian asymmetric: exp(-((x-μ)^2)/(2σ²)) with μ .25/.75.
- random smooth: sum of random cos/sine odd? Ensure nonnegative; normalize.
- random iid positive: rng.random(n) + small, normalize.
- random block: random weights for m blocks.

def multiplicative_search(p, cutoff, rng, alpha_base=0.1, tau_mode='ann', print_id=0):
    p = ensure_positive_sum1(p)
    best = p.copy(); best_score=score_from_conv(conv_self(p), len(p))
    n=len(p); L=next_fast_len(2*n)
    current = p.copy(); cur = (np.full(L,0), None)
    alpha = alpha_base
    tau_factor = 1.0
    stagnant=0
    it=0
    while time < cutoff:
       c=conv_self(current)
       m=max(c); cur_score=2*n*m
       if cur_score<best_score: best=current; best_score=cur_score
       # build weights
       if tau_factor <= 0: ...
       else lam = softmax
       Fp = np.fft.fft(current, L) # could compute before c? c from fft
       # Better compute Fp once, c from Fp
```

Let's implement robust helper functions:
```
import numpy as np
import time

def _clamp_nonnegative(x):
    x=np.asarray(x,dtype=np.float64)
    x=np.where(np.isfinite(x), x, 0.0)
    x=np.maximum(x,0.0)
    return x

def _normalize_sum1(p):
    s=p.sum()
    if s<=0 or not finite: return None
    return p/s

def _fast_len(m):
    return 1 << (int(np.ceil(np.log2(max(2,m+1)))))  # power of two
```
Need avoid log2 for m? use bit_length:
```
def _next_power2(m):
    if m <=1: return 1
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()
```
Convolution:
```
def _local_convolution(p):
    n=p.size
    L=_next_power2(2*n-1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, L)[:2*n-1]
    # tiny negative due roundoff; c = np.maximum(c,0)
    return c
def _local_score(p):
    p=_clamp_nonnegative(p)
    s=p.sum()
    if s < 1e-300: return np.inf
    n=p.size
    c=_local_convolution(p)
    mc=float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc<0: mc=0.0
    return float(2*n*mc/(s*s))
```
Using normalized p sum1, score=2n*max(c). Good.

Two-block analytic P:
```
def _two_block_pf(a,b,r):
    if r<=0: r=1e-12
    denom = a + r*b
    if denom<=0: return inf
    t1=a; t2=(r*r)*b; t3=2*r*a
    return max(t1,t2,t3)/(denom*denom)
```

Build two-block sequence normalized:
```
def _make_two_block(n,a,r):
    b=n-a
    U=1.0/(a + r*b)
    V=r*U
    seq=np.empty(n)
    seq[:a]=U; seq[a:]=V
    return seq
```

Golden function:
```
def _minimize_scalar(f, lo, hi, iters):
    invphi = (math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    x1 = hi - invphi*(hi-lo)
    x2 = lo + invphi*(hi-lo)
    f1=f(x1); f2=f(x2)
    for _ in range(iters):
       if f1 < f2:
          hi = x2; x2=x1; f2=f1; x1=hi - invphi*(hi-lo); f1=f(x1)
       else:
          lo = x1; x1=x2; f1=f2; x2=lo + invphi*(hi-lo); f2=f(x2)
    return (lo+hi)/2
```

Let's draft code:
```
import numpy as np
import time
import math

def _next_power2(m):
    m=int(max(1,m))
    return 1 << (m-1).bit_length()

def _local_convolution(p):
    n=p.size
    L=_next_power2(2*n-1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f, L)[:2*n-1]
    # protect against tiny imaginary? irfft real
    if c.size:
       # Roundoff can make small negatives, but max likely positive. We do max with zero only if all negative? For objective max, negative small not issue if max positive. But if p all tiny? min 0.
       c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return c
```
Clipping all negative to 0 changes max if all due p=0; p positive. Good. But clipping tiny negatives in c may slightly increase? no.

```
def _local_score(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if p.size==0: return float('inf')
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300: return inf
    n=p.size
    c=_local_convolution(p)
    mc=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc): return inf
    return float(2*n*mc/(s*s))
```
Since p positive, s ok.

`_two_block_pf(a,b,r):`
```
    if r <= 0: r=1e-300
    denom = float(a) + float(r)*float(b)
    if denom <=0: return inf
    t1=float(a)
    t2=float(r)*float(r)*float(b)
    t3=2.0*float(r)*float(a)
    return max(t1,t2,t3)/(denom*denom)
```
`a` int.

`_golden_analytic_r(a,b,iters=70):`
```
    lo=1e-4; hi=1e2
    gr=(math.sqrt(5.0)-1.0)/2.0
    x1=hi-gr*(hi-lo); x2=lo+gr*(hi-lo)
    f1=_two_block_pf(a,b,x1); f2=_two_block_pf(a,b,x2)
    for _ in range(iters):
       if f1 < f2: ...
    return 0.5*(lo+hi)
```
Range hi=100 maybe if a=b optimum r=2; if a small b huge r optimum 2a/b maybe <0.01? Could be if a=1,b=10000, r~0.0002; lo=1e-4 ok. If a/b >? a up to n/2, r maybe 2, hi=100 safe.

`_make_two_block(n,a,r):`
```
    b=n-a
    denom = float(a) + float(r)*float(b)
    U=1.0/denom
    V=r*U
    seq=np.empty(n,dtype=np.float64)
    seq[:a]=U
    seq[a:]=V
    return seq
```
If r maybe 0? no.

`_score_two_block_r(n,a,r):`
```
    seq=_make_two_block(n,a,r)
    return _local_score(seq)
```

`_best_two_block(n):`
```
    if n < 10:
       p=np.ones(n,dtype=np.float64)
       return p, _local_score(p)
    a_center=max(2, min(n-2, n//5))
    width=max(10, n//100)
    a_start=max(2,a_center-width)
    a_end=min(n-2,a_center+width)
    # ensure include at least a_center and n//5 maybe
    candidates=[]
    for a in range(a_start, a_end+1):
       if a > n//2: continue # only shorter first
       b=n-a
       r=_golden_analytic_r(a,b,50)
       sc=2*n*_two_block_pf(a,b,r)
       candidates.append((sc,a,r))
    if not candidates:
       a=max(2,min(n//2,n//5)); b=n-a; r=_golden_analytic_r(a,b,50); candidates=[(2*n*_two_block_pf(a,b,r),a,r)]
    candidates.sort()
    top_count=min(len(candidates), 15)
    best_seq=None; best_score=np.inf
    for _,a,r in candidates[:top_count]:
       # exact refinement
       r_exact=_golden_exact_r(n,a,60)
       # Also consider r from analytic maybe due nonunimodal boundary
       for rr in (r, _two_block_analytic_candidates? )
           seq=_make_two_block(n,a,rr); sc=_local_score(seq); update
       # Golden exact may have missed if boundaries make nonunimodal; evaluate initial r and some alternates
    # If all failed use uniform
    if best_seq is None:
       best_seq=_make_two_block(n, max(2,min(n//2,n//5)), 0.5)
       best_score=_local_score(best_seq)
    return best_seq, best_score
```
No lambdas; candidates tuple sort OK.

Full-grid gradient helper:
```
def _gradient_from_weights(p, lam, n, L):
    Fp = np.fft.fft(p, L)
    Flam = np.fft.fft(lam, L)
    h = np.fft.ifft(Flam * np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]
    idx = 2*np.arange(n)
    return h + p * lam[idx]
```
But Fp computed also for c. We can compute c and Fp in one function:
```
def _conv_and_fft(p,L):
    Fp=np.fft.fft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(Fp*Fp).real[:2*n-1]
    return Fp,c
```
Use full fft (not rfft) for both.

LSE weights:
```
def _soft_max_weights(c, tau):
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m <=0: return uniform
    if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <=0:
       tol = 1e-6*m + 1e-30
       active = c >= m - tol
       s = np.sum(active)
       if s==0: active = np.ones_like(c) ...
       return active/s
    w = np.exp((c-m)/tau)
    s=w.sum()
    if s==0 or not finite: fallback
    return w/s
```
Tau relative: `tau = c_scale * tau_factor`; c_scale = max(c) or 1/n.

Block update details with line search:
```
def _block_iterate_step(w, eta_list, c, lam, p, n, B, K):
   Fp = np.fft.fft(p, L) # maybe c already from Fp
   g = gradient...
   p_blk = p.reshape(K,B)
   wsum = p_blk.sum(axis=1)
   prod = (p_blk * g.reshape(K,B)).sum(axis=1)
   avg = np.divide(prod, wsum, out=np.zeros(K), where=wsum>0)
   current = expanded score
   best_w=w, best_future_score=inf
   for eta in eta_list:
      arg = -eta * n * avg  # eta dimensionless? Since avg~1/n, eta*n*avg~eta
      if max arg < -100? clip
      w2 = w * np.exp(np.clip(arg, -50, 0))
      s=w2.sum(); if s<=0 continue
      w2/=s
      p2=expand(w2)
      c2=conv_self(p2)
      sc=2*n*max(c2)
      if sc<best_future_score: store
   if best_future_score < inf: return w2,sc,c2
   else return w, current score
```

Loop:
```
while time.monotonic()<deadline:
   it+=1
   Fp,c = _convolution_fft(p,n)
   m=max(c); current_score=2*n*m
   if current_score<best_score: best...
   c_scale = m if m>0 else 1.0/n
   tau = c_scale * (0.2 + 0.8*max(0, 1 - it/500))? Maybe keep simple: eta schedule based on it:
   if it<20: tau_factor=1.0; elif it<100:0.5; elif it<300:0.3; else:0.15
   lam=_soft_max(c,tau_factor*c_scale)
   g=_gradient_from_fft(p,lam,Fp,n)
   g_blk = g.reshape(K,B)*p.reshape(K,B)
   prod = g_blk.sum(axis=1); psum=p.reshape(K,B).sum(axis=1)
   avg = np.divide(prod,psum,out=zeros,where=psum>0)
   # If avg max==0 break
   # line search: candidates
   best_cand_score = inf; best_w=None; best_p2=None
   current_base_score=current_score
   for s in step_list:
       exp_arg = -s * n * avg
       exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -50, 0)
       w2 = w * np.exp(exp_arg)
       if not finite or sum<=0: continue
       w2 = np.maximum(w2,1e-12/sum?) # floor
       w2 = w2/w2.sum()
       p2 = _expand_blocks(w2,B,K)
       c2 = _local_convolution(p2) # FFT
       sc2 = 2*n*max(c2)
       # store if sc2<best_cand_score
       if sc2<best_cand_score:
          best_cand_score=sc2; best_w=w2; best_p2=p2; best_c2=c2
   # accept if improves current or with temperature
   temp = 0.05 * max(0, 1 - it/1000)  # accept up to 5% worse initially
   if best_cand_score < current_score*(1+temp) or best_cand_score<current_score:
       w,current_p,c2? = best_w,best_p2,best_c2
       current_score=best_cand_score
       stagnant=0
   else:
       stagnant +=1
   if stagnant > 50:
       # perturb w
       noise=0.05
       w = w*np.exp(rng.normal(0,noise,K)); w/=w.sum()
       p=_expand_blocks(w,B,K)
       stagnant=0
   if it % 200 ==0: print progress
```

def _random_fourier_weights(K, rng, num_modes=8, amp_scale=0.3):
    x = ... centers
    w = np.ones(K)
    for m in range(1,num_modes+1):
       amp = rng.uniform(0.0, amp_scale) * (1.0/m**0.5)
       phase = 0.0 # sine only (odd) ; even sine modes also odd
       w += amp * np.sin(2*np.pi*m*x)
    w = np.maximum(w, 0.01)
    # also ensure mean? Not necessary, normalize; but maybe subtract mean after to enhance? w = w - (w.mean()-1); clip
    return w/w.sum()
```

Let's design `_score_from_p` robust:
```
def _score_from_p(p):
    p=np.asarray(p,dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim !=1 or p.size==0: return inf
    p=np.where(np.isfinite(p),p,0.0)
    p=np.maximum(p,0.0)
    s=float(p.sum())
    if s <= 1e-300: return inf
    n=p.size
    L=_next_power2(2*n-1)
    f=np.fft.rfft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.irfft(f*f,L)[:2*n-1]
    if c.size==0: mc=0.0
    else: mc=float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc <0: mc=0.0
    return float(2.0*n*mc/(s*s))
```
Clipping c negatives: A tiny negative max only if all negative; mc=0. For overflow? no.

`_convolution_fft_full`:
```
def _convolution_fft_full(p):
    n=p.size; L=_next_power2(2*n-1)
    Fp=np.fft.fft(p,L)
    c=np.fft.ifft(Fp*Fp).real[:2*n-1]
    # do not clip? For score, max may be tiny negative due roundoff; use np.maximum? fine.
    c=np.maximum(c,0.0)
    return Fp,c
```

`_gradient_from_weights`:
```
def _gradient_from_weights(p, lam, Fp):
    n=p.size; L=Fp.size
    Flam=np.fft.fft(lam,L)
    h=np.fft.ifft(Flam * np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]
    return h + p * lam[:2*n-1][2*np.arange(n)]
```
Need lam length 2n-1. `lam[:2*n-1]` okay.

`_softmax_weights`:
```
def _softmax_weights(c, scale_tau):
    m=float(np.max(c)); sz=c.size
    if m <=0 or sz==0:
       return np.full(sz,1.0/sz)
    if scale_tau <=0:
       tol=1e-6*m+1e-30
       w=(c >= m-tol).astype(float); s=sum; if s==0: ...; return w/s
    tau=m*scale_tau
    vals=(c-m)/tau
    # to prevent overflow, vals <=0; exp
    w=np.exp(vals)
    s=w.sum()
    if not finite or s==0: active fallback
    return w/s
```
scale_tau values: if 0 active.

def _expand_blocks(w,B,K):
    n=K*B
    return np.repeat(w/float(B), B).astype(np.float64, copy=False)
```

def _initial_block_weights(K, kind, rng=None):
   x=np.linspace(0,1,K,endpoint=False)
   if kind==0: w=np.ones(K)
   elif kind==1: w=0.2+0.8*x

   elif kind==3: w=1.0+0.75*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)
   elif kind==4: w=1.0+0.7*np.sin(2*np.pi*x)+0.5*np.sin(4*np.pi*x)
   elif kind==5: w=np.where(x<0.2, 1.8, 0.6)
   elif kind==6: w=np.where(x<0.1,2.0, np.where(x>=0.9,0.2,1.0))? etc

   elif kind==8: w=0.2+ np.exp? 
   else random fourier
   w=np.maximum(w,1e-4); w=w.sum(); return w
   # for random fourier, use rng
```

```python
"""
Search for a low-peak non-negative step-function height sequence.

Algorithm:
- Use many short step profiles as initialization seeds.
- Optimize piecewise-constant profiles with a multiplicative gradient method.
  The gradient is computed from an LSE soft-max of the full convolution
  coefficients, not from the evaluator, so candidates can be evaluated cheaply
  by FFT.
- Refine the best expanded profile with a full-coordinate multiplicative update.
- The authoritative evaluator is checked once at the end.
"""

import numpy as np
import time
import math


def _next_power2(m):
    m = int(max(1, m))
    return 1 << (m - 1).bit_length()


def _score_from_p(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return float("inf")
    p = np.where(np.isfinite(p), p, 0.0)
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 1e-300:
        return float("inf")
    n = p.size
    L = _next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    f = np.fft.rfft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.irfft(f * f, L)[:2 * n - 1]
    if c.size == 0:
        mc = 0.0
    else:
        c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
        mc = float(np.max(c))
    if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
        mc = 0.0
    return float(2.0 * n * mc / (s * s))


def _convolution_fft_full(p):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    n = p.size
    L = _next_power2(2 * n - 1)
    Fp = np.fft.fft(p, L)
    c = np.fft.ifft(Fp * Fp).real[:2 * n - 1]
    c = np.maximum(c, 0.0)
    return Fp, c


def _gradient_from_weights(p, lam, Fp):
    n = p.size
    L = Fp.size
    Flam = np.fft.fft(lam, L)
    h = np.fft.ifft(Flam * np.conj(Fp)).real[:n]
    idx = 2 * np.arange(n)
    return h + p * lam[idx]


def _softmax_weights(c, tau_scale):
    sz = c.size
    if sz == 0:
        return np.array([], dtype=np.float64)
    m = float(np.max(c))
    if m <= 0.0:
        return np.full(sz, 1.0 / sz, dtype=np.float64)
    if tau_scale <= 0.0:
        tol = 1e-6 * m + 1e-30
        w = (c >= m - tol).astype(np.float64)
        s = float(np.sum(w))
        if s <= 0.0:
            w = np.zeros(sz, dtype=np.float64)
            w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
            return w
        return w / s

    tau = tau_scale * m
    vals = (c - m) / tau
    w = np.exp(vals)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        w = np.zeros(sz, dtype=np.float64)
        w[int(np.argmax(c))] = 1.0
        return w
    return w / s


def _make_two_block(n, a, r):
    if a <= 0 or a >= n:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    b = n - a
    denom = float(a) + float(r) * float(b)
    if denom <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    U = 1.0 / denom
    V = r * U
    p = np.empty(n, dtype=np.float64)
    p[:a] = U
    p[a:] = V
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s > 0.0:
        p /= s
    return p


def _expand_blocks(w, B, K):
    p = np.repeat(np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64) / float(B), B)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s > 0.0:
        p /= s
    return p


def _initial_block_weights(K, kind, rng=None):
    if rng is None:
        rng = np.random.default_rng(123456789)
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, K, endpoint=False)

    if kind == 0:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    elif kind == 1:
        w = 0.15 + 0.85 * x
    elif kind == 2:
        w = 0.15 + 0.85 * (1.0 - x)
    elif kind == 3:
        w = 1.0 + 0.75 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    elif kind == 4:
        w = (
            1.0
            + 0.65 * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * x)
            + 0.45 * np.sin(4.0 * np.pi * x)
            + 0.25 * np.sin(6.0 * np.pi * x)
        )
    elif kind == 5:
        w = np.where(x < 0.2, 1.7, 0.7)
    elif kind == 6:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        lo = max(1, K // 12)
        hi = max(lo + 1, K - K // 12)
        w[:lo] = 2.0
        w[-K // 12:] = 0.25
        w[lo:hi] = 0.8
    elif kind == 7:
        w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))
    elif kind == 8:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        for m in range(1, 7):
            amp = rng.uniform(-0.6, 0.6) / math.sqrt(float(m))
            w = w + amp * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * float(m) * x)
    elif kind == 9:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        w = w + 0.5 * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * x)
    else:
        w = rng.dirichlet(np.ones(K))

    w = np.asarray(w, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-5)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(K)
    return w / s


def _block_average_gradient(p, g, B, K):
    blk_p = p.reshape(K, B)
    blk_g = g.reshape(K, B)
    prod = np.sum(blk_p * blk_g, axis=1)
    psum = np.sum(blk_p, axis=1)
    out = np.zeros(K, dtype=np.float64)
    np.divide(prod, psum, out=out, where=psum > 1e-300)
    return out


def _optimize_blocks(n, K, w0, deadline, print_id=0):
    if n <= 0 or K <= 0:
        return None, None, float("inf")
    B = n // K
    if B < 1:
        B = 1
        K = n
    n = K * B

    w = np.asarray(w0, dtype=np.float64)
    if w.size != K:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
    w = np.where(np.isfinite(w), w, 0.0)
    w = np.maximum(w, 1e-8)
    s = float(np.sum(w))
    if s <= 0.0:
        w = np.ones(K, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(K)
    w /= s

    p = _expand_blocks(w, B, K)
    best_w = w.copy()
    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_from_p(p)

    current_p = p.copy()
    current_w = w.copy()
    current_score = best_score
    rng = np.random.default_rng(100000 + print_id * 997 + K)
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    steps = (0.03, 0.07, 0.14, 0.28, 0.55, 1.1)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        Fp, c = _convolution_fft_full(current_p)
        mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
            mc = 0.0
        current_score = float(2.0 * n * mc)
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_w = current_w.copy()
            best_p = current_p.copy()

        if it < 50:
            tau_scale = 1.0
        elif it < 200:
            tau_scale = 0.55
        elif it < 700:
            tau_scale = 0.32
        elif it < 1800:
            tau_scale = 0.20
        else:
            tau_scale = 0.12

        lam = _softmax_weights(c, tau_scale)
        g = _gradient_from_weights(current_p, lam, Fp)
        avg = _block_average_gradient(current_p, g, B, K)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(avg)) or float(np.max(np.abs(avg))) <= 0.0:
            break

        best_cand_score = float("inf")
        best_cand_w = None
        best_cand_p = None

        for st in steps:
            exp_arg = -float(st) * float(n) * avg
            exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -50.0, 0.0)
            w2 = current_w * np.exp(exp_arg)
            w2 = np.where(np.isfinite(w2), w2, 0.0)
            w2 = np.maximum(w2, 1e-15 / float(K))
            sw = float(np.sum(w2))
            if sw <= 0.0:
                continue
            w2 /= sw
            p2 = _expand_blocks(w2, B, K)
            sc2 = _score_from_p(p2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc2
                best_cand_w = w2
                best_cand_p = p2

        accepted = False
        temp = 0.08 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(it) / 600.0)
        if (
            best_cand_w is not None
            and np.isfinite(best_cand_score)
            and (best_cand_score < current_score * (1.0 + temp) or best_cand_score < best_score)
        ):
            current_w = best_cand_w
            current_p = best_cand_p
            current_score = best_cand_score
            stagnant = 0
            accepted = True
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_w = current_w.copy()
                best_p = current_p.copy()

        if not accepted:
            stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 40 or (it % 1200 == 0):
            noise = 0.07 if stagnant <= 120 else 0.12
            w2 = current_w * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, K))
            w2 = np.where(np.isfinite(w2), w2, 0.0)
            w2 = np.maximum(w2, 1e-12 / float(K))
            sw = float(np.sum(w2))
            if sw > 0.0:
                w2 /= sw
                current_w = w2
                current_p = _expand_blocks(w2, B, K)
            stagnant = 0

        if it % 700 == 0:
            print(
                "block_search id={} K={} it={} current={:.6f} best={:.6f}".format(
                    print_id,
                    K,
                    it,
                    current_score,
                    best_score,
                )
            )

    if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
        pass
    return best_w, best_p, best_score


def _full_refine(p, deadline, rng, n=None):
    p = np.asarray(p, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim != 1 or p.size == 0:
        return p, float("inf")
    n = p.size
    p = np.maximum(p, 0.0)
    s = float(np.sum(p))
    if s <= 0.0:
        return p, float("inf")
    p /= s

    best_p = p.copy()
    best_score = _score_from_p(p)
    current_p = p.copy()
    current_score = best_score
    stagnant = 0
    it = 0
    steps = (0.02, 0.05, 0.10, 0.20, 0.35)

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        it += 1
        Fp, c = _convolution_fft_full(current_p)
        mc = float(np.max(c)) if c.size else 0.0
        if not np.isfinite(mc) or mc < 0.0:
            mc = 0.0
        current_score = float(2.0 * n * mc)
        if current_score < best_score:
            best_score = current_score
            best_p = current_p.copy()

        if it < 40:
            tau_scale = 0.5
        elif it < 200:
            tau_scale = 0.3
        elif it < 800:
            tau_scale = 0.2
        else:
            tau_scale = 0.13

        lam = _softmax_weights(c, tau_scale)
        g = _gradient_from_weights(current_p, lam, Fp)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(g)):
            break

        best_cand_score = float("inf")
        best_cand_p = None

        for st in steps:
            exp_arg = -float(st) * float(n) * g
            exp_arg = np.clip(exp_arg, -50.0, 0.0)
            p2 = current_p * np.exp(exp_arg)
            p2 = np.where(np.isfinite(p2), p2, 0.0)
            if float(np.min(p2)) <= 0.0:
                p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-15 / float(n))
            sp = float(np.sum(p2))
            if sp <= 0.0:
                continue
            p2 /= sp
            sc2 = _score_from_p(p2)
            if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_cand_score:
                best_cand_score = sc2
                best_cand_p = p2

        accepted = False
        temp = 0.04 * max(0.0, 1.0 - float(it) / 500.0)
        if (
            best_cand_p is not None
            and np.isfinite(best_cand_score)
            and (best_cand_score < current_score * (1.0 + temp) or best_cand_score < best_score)
        ):
            current_p = best_cand_p
            current_score = best_cand_score
            stagnant = 0
            accepted = True
            if current_score < best_score:
                best_score = current_score
                best_p = current_p.copy()

        if not accepted:
            stagnant += 1

        if stagnant > 35 or (it % 1000 == 0):
            noise = 0.006 if stagnant <= 120 else 0.012
            p2 = current_p * np.exp(rng.normal(0.0, noise, n))
            p2 = np.where(np.isfinite(p2), p2, 0.0)
            p2 = np.maximum(p2, 1e-15 / float(n))
            sp = float(np.sum(p2))
            if sp > 0.0:
                current_p = p2 / sp
            stagnant = 0

        if it % 400 == 0:
            print("full_refine it={} current={:.6f} best={:.6f}".format(it, current_score, best_score))

    return best_p, best_score


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Return a nonnegative sequence with a small evaluator score.
    """
    t0 = time.monotonic()
    deadline = t0 + max(0.0, float(budget_s) - 8.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    # Use a moderate length: fast enough for final authoritative evaluation
    # and large enough that the asymptotic step-function behavior is visible.
    n = 4096

    best_p = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    best_score = _score_from_p(best_p)
    print("initial uniform score {:.8f}".format(best_score))

    # Deterministic early candidates: two-block step shapes.
    fracs = (0.08, 0.12, 0.16, 0.20, 0.24, 0.28, 0.32, 0.38)
    rs = (0.15, 0.25, 0.35, 0.5, 0.75, 1.0, 1.4, 2.0)
    for fr in fracs:
        a = int(round(fr * float(n)))
        if a <= 1 or a >= n - 1:
            continue
        for rr in rs:
            p = _make_two_block(n, a, rr)
            sc = _score_from_p(p)
            if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
                best_score = sc
                best_p = p.copy()
    print("after fixed two-block candidates best={:.8f}".format(best_score))

    # Block-profile search phases.  K is the number of constant blocks.
    phase_specs = (
        (8, 1, 80, 0),
        (8, 3, 80, 0),
        (8, 8, 80, 1),
        (16, None, 120, None),
        (16, 4, 120, 0),
        (16, 8, 120, 2),
        (32, None, 150, None),
        (32, 1, 120, 0),
        (32, 8, 150, 3),
        (32, 7, 120, 4),
        (64, None, 200, None),
        (64, 8, 170, 5),
        (128, None, 180, None),
    )

    for K, kind, secs, kid in phase_specs:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        phase_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + float(secs))
        if time.monotonic() >= phase_deadline:
            continue

        if kind is None:
            B0 = n // K
            if B0 > 0 and best_p is not None:
                w0 = best_p.reshape(K, B0).mean(axis=1)
                if not np.all(np.isfinite(w0)) or float(np.min(w0)) <= 0.0:
                    w0 = _initial_block_weights(K, 1, rng)
            else:
                w0 = _initial_block_weights(K, 1, rng)
        else:
            w0 = _initial_block_weights(K, int(kind), rng)

        res_w, res_p, res_sc = _optimize_blocks(n, K, w0, phase_deadline, print_id=1000 * K + (kind if kind is not None else -1))
        if res_p is not None and np.isfinite(res_sc) and res_sc < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = res_sc
            best_p = res_p.copy()

        print(
            "phase K={} kind={} timing_deadline_left={:.1f}s best={:.8f}".format(
                K,
                kind,
                max(0.0, deadline - time.monotonic()),
                best_score,
            )
        )

    # Final full-coordinate refinement.
    if time.monotonic() < deadline:
        refine_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + 430.0)
        refined_p, refined_sc = _full_refine(best_p, refine_deadline, rng, n)
        if refined_sc < best_score:
            best_score = refined_sc
            best_p = refined_p.copy()
        print("after full refine best={:.8f}".format(best_score))

    out_list = [float(x) for x in best_p]

    # Authoritative check.  If something unexpected happens, fall back to uniform.
    try:
        exact = float(evaluate_sequence(out_list))
    except Exception:
        exact = best_score

    if not np.isfinite(exact):
        out_list = [1.0] * n
        exact = 2.0

    if exact > best_score + 0.2:
        uniform = [1.0] * n
        try:
            un_exact = float(evaluate_sequence(uniform))
        except Exception:
            un_exact = 2.0
        if np.isfinite(un_exact) and un_exact < exact:
            out_list = uniform

    print(
        "final returned length={} local_best={:.8f} exact={:.8f} elapsed={:.1f}s".format(
            len(out_list),
            best_score,
            exact,
            time.monotonic() - t0,
        )
    )
    return out_list
```
